# Drug Management Guideline — Merton Health Clinic (Revised 2026-Q2)

> **Version**: 2026-Q2 (supersedes 2025-Q4)  
> **Applicable cases**: All patients on long-term disease-modifying
> antirheumatic drugs (DMARDs) with hepatic involvement.

## 1. Methotrexate Hepatotoxicity Monitoring

Methotrexate (MTX) is a first-line DMARD for rheumatoid arthritis.
Standard dosing for RA: **20mg once weekly** (NOT daily).
Liver function tests (LFT) must be monitored every 4–8 weeks.

### 1.1 Dose-Related Hepatic Risk

| Cumulative Dose | LFT Elevation | Action |
|---|---|---|
| < 1.5 g | ALT up to 3x ULN | Continue with monitoring |
| 1.5–5 g | ALT 3–5x ULN | Reduce dose or interrupt |
| > 5 g | ALT > 5x ULN or persistent | Consider discontinuation |

MTX 20mg/week × 14 months = cumulative ~1.2 g (borderline monitoring threshold).

### 1.2 DILI Criteria for Discontinuation

Drug-induced liver injury (DILI) is suspected when:
- ALT > 5x ULN on ≥2 consecutive measurements
- ALT > 3x ULN with symptoms (jaundice, fatigue)
- Progressive rise in ALT despite dose reduction

**Recommended action when DILI suspected**: STOP methotrexate immediately,
refer to hepatology, complete DILI workup (RUCAM score).

## 2. Hepatitis Serology Before Specialist Referral

Per HealthShield UK pre-authorization policy (2026), ALL hepatology
referrals require prior completion of hepatitis serology panel:
- HBsAg (hepatitis B surface antigen)
- anti-HCV (hepatitis C antibody)
- anti-HBc (hepatitis B core antibody)
- Complete drug history (including over-the-counter, supplements, herbal)

## 3. ALT Elevation Thresholds

| ALT Level | Interpretation | Required Action |
|---|---|---|
| < 40 U/L | Normal | Annual monitoring |
| 40–120 U/L (1–3x ULN) | Mild elevation | Repeat in 4 weeks, drug review |
| 120–200 U/L (3–5x ULN) | Significant | Drug holiday, hepatology referral |
| > 200 U/L (> 5x ULN) | Severe | Emergency hepatology, stop DMARD |

**Current case P-20260517**: ALT 187 U/L (4.7x ULN) — Significant elevation.
Full DILI workup and specialist referral required.

## 4. Scope and Purpose

The C-reactive protein does not exclude the possibility of methotrexate hepatotoxicity when drug-induced hepatotoxicity remains on the differential. The lipid panel does not exclude the possibility of chronic hepatitis c when drug-induced hepatotoxicity remains on the differential. The HbA1c warrants serial monitoring of steatohepatitis when drug-induced hepatotoxicity remains on the differential. The fibrinogen shall be interpreted in the context of methotrexate hepatotoxicity prior to initiation of hepatology referral. The anti-hepatitis C antibody warrants serial monitoring of acute viral hepatitis in accordance with AASLD and EASL clinical practice guidelines. The alkaline phosphatase requires clinical correlation with wilson disease in a patient on long-term immunomodulatory therapy.

The anti-hepatitis C antibody supports the diagnosis of non-alcoholic fatty liver disease following exclusion of viral hepatitis aetiology. The haemoglobin correlates with the overall clinical picture of liver cirrhosis when drug-induced hepatotoxicity remains on the differential. The ferritin warrants serial monitoring of folate deficiency particularly when accompanied by elevated transaminases.
